user
Design v168 from v160 using a materially different numerical primitive. Do not inspect xpuoj/ or old fused_moe_gemm/.
Baseline guarded SwiGLU uses `gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up`. Historical routed v041 used `gate / (1 + T.exp(-gate)) * up`; v043 native sigmoid was only locally/C500 slower, but C500 timing is not valid performance evidence and packed-dense OJ never isolated this primitive. Test E64 only with explicit natural-exp sigmoid while E16/E32 retain v160. Do not use exp2 approximation.
Assess exact expression, fp16/fp32 promotion, error tolerance, overflow behavior for negative/positive gate, novelty, and potential MACA lowering differences. This numerical primitive direction is justified once; no approximation variants unless positive. Output HISTORICAL_EVIDENCE, SELECTED_EXPERIMENT, MODIFICATION_POINTS, NUMERICAL_PROOF, SHAPE_ISOLATION, EXPECTED_SIGNAL, RISKS, IMPLEMENTATION, FINAL_GATE PASS_TO_IMPLEMENT or BLOCKED.

> v043_codex_verify.txt:527:.\contest_fused_moe_gemm\problem_snapshot.json:50:        "text": "```python\nimport torch\
n\ndef run_kernel(\n    stacked_expert_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n    gate_w: torch.Tensor,\n    up_w: torch.Tensor,\n    d
own_w: torch.Tensor,\n    routed_expert_weights: torch.Tensor,\n    group_sizes: torch.Tensor,\n    group_offsets: torc
h.Tensor,\n    group_padded_offsets: torch.Tensor,\n    group_idx_for_bx: torch.Tensor,\n    out: torch.Tensor,\n) -> N
one:\n    out.zero_()\n    num_experts = int(group_sizes.numel())\n\n    x_f32 = stacked_expert_tokens.to(torch.float32
)\n    gate_f32 = gate_w.to(torch.float32)\n    up_f32 = up_w.to(torch.float32)\n    down_f32 = down_w.to(torch.float32
)\n\n    for expert_idx in range(num_experts):\n        valid_count = int(group_sizes[expert_idx].item())\n        if v
alid_count == 0:\n            continue\n\n        raw_start = int(group_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n        padded_sta
rt = int(group_padded_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n\n        x_e = x_f32[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count]\n    
    gate_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, gate_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        up_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, up_f32
[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        hidden_act = torch.sigmoid(gate_logits) * gate_logits * up_logits\n        y_e =
 torch.matmul(hidden_act, down_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        y_e *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start:raw_sta
rt + valid_count].to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(1)\n\n        out[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count].copy_(y_e.to(t
orch.float16))\n```\n\n这份参考实现对应的数学语义是：\n\n```text\nfor each expert e:\n    x_e = stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start : p
added_start + valid_count]\n\n    gate_logits = x_e @ gate_w[e]^T\n    up_logits   = x_e @ up_w[e]^T\n    hidden      =
 silu(gate_logits) * up_logits\n    y_e         = hidden @ down_w[e]^T\n    y_e        *= routed_expert_weights[raw_sta
rt : raw_start + valid_count]\n\n    out[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count] = y_e\n```\n\n其中：\n\n- `raw_start =
 group_offsets[e]`\n- `padded_start = group_padded_offsets[e]`\n- `valid_count = group_sizes[e]`\n\n实现要点：\n\n**该题目的 GPU
 计算算子不能使用 PyTorch，请只用 TileLang 完成相关计算算子。**  \n\n1. `group_offsets` 和 `group_padded_offsets` 需要分别使用，不能混用。\n2. `routed_ex
pert_weights` 的索引基于真实 token 顺序。\n3. `stacked_expert_tokens` 与 `out` 的索引基于 padding 后顺序。\n4. padding 行不参与计算，输出保持为 `0`。\n5
. 当前 baseline 主路径会把 `group_idx_for_bx` 用作 block 到 expert group 的映射。\n6. 为了对齐 TileLang kernel 的常见实现，最终结果按 `float16` 写回。\

e_gemm\problem_snapshot.json:50:        "text": "```python\nimport torch\n\ndef run_kernel(\n    stacked_expert_tokens:
 torch.Tensor,\n    gate_w: torch.Tensor,\n    up_w: torch.Tensor,\n    down_w: torch.Tensor,\n    routed_expert_weight
s: torch.Tensor,\n    group_sizes: torch.Tensor,\n    group_offsets: torch.Tensor,\n    group_padded_offsets: torch.Ten
sor,\n    group_idx_for_bx: torch.Tensor,\n    out: torch.Tensor,\n) -> None:\n    out.zero_()\n    num_experts = int(g
roup_sizes.numel())\n\n    x_f32 = stacked_expert_tokens.to(torch.float32)\n    gate_f32 = gate_w.to(torch.float32)\n  
  up_f32 = up_w.to(torch.float32)\n    down_f32 = down_w.to(torch.float32)\n\n    for expert_idx in range(num_experts):
\n        valid_count = int(group_sizes[expert_idx].item())\n        if valid_count == 0:\n            continue\n\n    
    raw_start = int(group_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n        padded_start = int(group_padded_offsets[expert_idx].item
())\n\n        x_e = x_f32[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count]\n        gate_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, gate_f32[e
xpert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        up_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, up_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        hidden_
act = torch.sigmoid(gate_logits) * gate_logits * up_logits\n        y_e = torch.matmul(hidden_act, down_f32[expert_idx]
.transpose(0, 1))\n        y_e *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start:raw_start + valid_count].to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(
1)\n\n        out[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count].copy_(y_e.to(torch.float16))\n```\n\n这份参考实现对应的数学语义是：\n\n```t
ext\nfor each expert e:\n    x_e = stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count]\n\n    gate_logits 
= x_e @ gate_w[e]^T\n    up_logits   = x_e @ up_w[e]^T\n    hidden      = silu(gate_logits) * up_logits\n    y_e       
  = hidden @ down_w[e]^T\n    y_e        *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start : raw_start + valid_count]\n\n    out[padde
d_start : padded_start + valid_count] = y_e\n```\n\n其中：\n\n- `raw_start = group_offsets[e]`\n- `padded_start = group_pa
dded_offsets[e]`\n- `valid_count = group_sizes[e]`\n\n实现要点：\n\n**该题目的 GPU 计算算子不能使用 PyTorch，请只用 TileLang 完成相关计算算子。**  \n
\n1. `group_offsets` 和 `group_padded_offsets` 需要分别使用，不能混用。\n2. `routed_expert_weights` 的索引基于真实 token 顺序。\n3. `stacked_e
xpert_tokens` 与 `out` 的索引基于 padding 后顺序。\n4. padding 行不参与计算，输出保持为 `0`。\n5. 当前 baseline 主路径会把 `group_idx_for_bx` 用作 bloc
k 到 expert group 的映射。\n6. 为了对齐 TileLang kernel 的常见实现，最终结果按 `float16` 写回。\n\n"

roup_sizes.numel())\n\n    x_f32 = stacked_expert_tokens.to(torch.float32)\n    gate_f32 = gate_w.to(torch.float32)\n  
  up_f32 = up_w.to(torch.float32)\n    down_f32 = down_w.to(torch.float32)\n\n    for expert_idx in range(num_experts):
\n        valid_count = int(group_sizes[expert_idx].item())\n        if valid_count == 0:\n            continue\n\n    
    raw_start = int(group_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n        padded_start = int(group_padded_offsets[expert_idx].item
())\n\n        x_e = x_f32[padded_start:padded_start + valid_count]\n        gate_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, gate_f32[e
xpert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        up_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, up_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        hidden_
act = torch.sigmoid(gate_logits) * gate_logits * up_logits\n        y_e = torch.matmul(hidden_act, down_f32[expert_idx]
.transpose(0, 1))\n        y_e *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start:raw_start + valid_count].to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(

ext\nfor each expert e:\n    x_e = stacked_expert_tokens[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count]\n\n    gate_logits 
= x_e @ gate_w[e]^T\n    up_logits   = x_e @ up_w[e]^T\n    hidden      = silu(gate_logits) * up_logits\n    y_e       
  = hidden @ down_w[e]^T\n    y_e        *= routed_expert_weights[raw_start : raw_start + valid_count]\n\n    out[padde
d_start : padded_start + valid_count] = y_e\n```\n\n其中：\n\n- `raw_start = group_offsets[e]`\n- `padded_start = group_pa
dded_offsets[e]`\n- `valid_count = group_sizes[e]`\n\n实现要点：\n\n**该题目的 GPU 计算算子不能使用 PyTorch，请只用 TileLang 完成相关计算算子。**  \n
\n1. `group_offsets` 和 `group_padded_offsets` 需要分别使用，不能混用。\n2. `routed_expert_weights` 的索引基于真实 token 顺序。\n3. `stacked_e
xpert_tokens` 与 `out` 的索引基于 padding 后顺序。\n4. padding 行不参与计算，输出保持为 `0`。\n5. 当前 baseline 主路径会把 `group_idx_for_bx` 用作 bloc
k 到 expert group 的映射。\n6. 为了对齐 TileLang kernel 的常见实现，最终结果按 `float16` 写回。\n\n"

: "```python\nimport torch\n\ndef run_kernel(\n    stacked_expert_tokens: torch.Tensor,\n    gate_w: torch.Tensor,\n   
 up_w: torch.Tensor,\n    down_w: torch.Tensor,\n    routed_expert_weights: torch.Tensor,\n    group_sizes: torch.Tenso
r,\n    group_offsets: torch.Tensor,\n    group_padded_offsets: torch.Tensor,\n    group_idx_for_bx: torch.Tensor,\n   
 out: torch.Tensor,\n) -> None:\n    out.zero_()\n    num_experts = int(group_sizes.numel())\n\n    x_f32 = stacked_exp
ert_tokens.to(torch.float32)\n    gate_f32 = gate_w.to(torch.float32)\n    up_f32 = up_w.to(torch.float32)\n    down_f3
2 = down_w.to(torch.float32)\n\n    for expert_idx in range(num_experts):\n        valid_count = int(group_sizes[expert
_idx].item())\n        if valid_count == 0:\n            continue\n\n        raw_start = int(group_offsets[expert_idx].
item())\n        padded_start = int(group_padded_offsets[expert_idx].item())\n\n        x_e = x_f32[padded_start:padded
_start + valid_count]\n        gate_logits = torch.matmul(x_e, gate_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        up_logits
 = torch.matmul(x_e, up_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        hidden_act = torch.sigmoid(gate_logits) * gate_logits
 * up_logits\n        y_e = torch.matmul(hidden_act, down_f32[expert_idx].transpose(0, 1))\n        y_e *= routed_exper
t_weights[raw_start:raw_start + valid_count].to(torch.float32).unsqueeze(1)\n\n        out[padded_start:padded_start + 
valid_count].copy_(y_e.to(torch.float16))\n```\n\n这份参考实现对应的数学语义是：\n\n```text\nfor each expert e:\n    x_e = stacked_exp
ert_tokens[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count]\n\n    gate_logits = x_e @ gate_w[e]^T\n    up_logits   = x_e @ u
p_w[e]^T\n    hidden      = silu(gate_logits) * up_logits\n    y_e         = hidden @ down_w[e]^T\n    y_e        *= ro
uted_expert_weights[raw_start : raw_start + valid_count]\n\n    out[padded_start : padded_start + valid_count] = y_e\n`
``\n\n其中：\n\n- `raw_start = group_offsets[e]`\n- `padded_start = group_padded_offsets[e]`\n- `valid_count = group_sizes
[e]`\n\n实现要点：\n\n**该题目的 GPU 计算算子不能使用 PyTorch，请只用 TileLang 完成相关计算算子。**  \n\n1. `group_offsets` 和 `group_padded_offsets` 
需要分别使用，不能混用。\n2. `routed_expert_weights` 的索引基于真实 token 顺序。\n3. `stacked_expert_tokens` 与 `out` 的索引基于 padding 后顺序。\n4. p
adding 行不参与计算，输出保持为 `0`。\n5. 当前 baseline 主路径会把 `group_idx_for_bx` 用作 block 到 expert group 的映射。\n6. 为了对齐 TileLang kernel
 的常见实现，最终结果按 `float16` 写回。\n\n"

Design v168 from v160 using a materially different numerical primitive. Do not inspect xpuoj/ or old fused_moe_gemm/.
Baseline guarded SwiGLU uses `gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up`. Historical routed v041 used `gate / (1 + T.exp(-gate)) * up`; v043 native sigmoid was only locally/C500 slower, but C500 timing is not valid performance evidence and packed-dense OJ never isolated this primitive. Test E64 only with explicit natural-exp sigmoid while E16/E32 retain v160. Do not use exp2 approximation.
Assess exact expression, fp16/fp32 promotion, error tolerance, overflow behavior for negative/positive gate, novelty, and potential MACA lowering differences. This numerical primitive direction is justified once; no approximation variants unless positive. Output HISTORICAL_EVIDENCE, SELECTED_EXPERIMENT, MODIFICATION_POINTS, NUMERICAL_PROOF, SHAPE_ISOLATION, EXPECTED_SIGNAL, RISKS, IMPLEMENTATION, FINAL_GATE PASS_TO_IMPLEMENT or BLOCKED.

codex
**HISTORICAL_EVIDENCE**

v160 accepted with native guarded SwiGLU:

`gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up`

Relevant current timings from `v160_xpuoj_detail.json`:
- E16: `2.018-2.024 ms`
- E32: `3.673 ms`
- E64: `6.557 ms`
- overall display score: `83.67`

Historical v041 used explicit natural exp:

`gate / (1.0 + T.exp(-gate)) * up`

v041 and v043 both accepted, but both were packed-dense era submissions around baseline speed:
- v041 E64: `35.618 ms`
- v043 E64 native sigmoid: `35.614 ms`

So historical evidence proves correctness viability, not current performance. It does not isolate the primitive in v160’s routed/packed-expert structure.

**SELECTED_EXPERIMENT**

Create v168 from [v160_e16_fc1_sequential_free.py](D:/C_data/EurakaOpts/contest_fused_moe_gemm/v160_e16_fc1_sequential_free.py:105).

Only E64 changes from native sigmoid to explicit natural-exp sigmoid. E16/E32 retain v160 exactly.

Use:

```python
gate / (1.0 + T.exp(-gate)) * up
```

Do not use `exp2`, polynomial, reciprocal approximation, clamp, or alternate stable sigmoid.

**MODIFICATION_POINTS**

1. Add a separate `_swiglu_exp_kernel(...)` cloned from `_swiglu_kernel`.
2. In that kernel, replace only:

```python
gate * T.sigmoid(gate) * up_output[...]
```

with:

```python
(gate / (1.0 + T.exp(-gate))) * up_output[...]
```

3. Extend `_get_kernels(...)` to return both native and exp SwiGLU kernels, or return `swiglu_exp` only when `num_experts == 64`.
4. In `run_kernel(...)`, dispatch:
   - `num_experts == 64`: use exp kernel
   - `num_experts == 16 or 32`: use existing native `swiglu`

No FC1 order, workspace, expert chunking, pack, down GEMM, or unpack changes.

**NUMERICAL_PROOF**

Mathematically:

```text
sigmoid(g) = 1 / (1 + exp(-g))
silu(g) = g * sigmoid(g)
        = g / (1 + exp(-g))
```

So the selected expression is mathematically equivalent to baseline SwiGLU before finite-precision effects.

Promotion assessment:
- v160 stores `gate_output` and `up_output` as fp16 tensors before activation.
- `gate = gate_output[...]` is therefore fp16 at the TileLang scalar boundary unless TileLang promotes internally.
- The experiment should not add an explicit fp32 cast because that would test both primitive and precision promotion at once.
- Constants `1.0` may cause TileLang to promote parts of the expression; this is acceptable as part of the natural-exp lowering being tested, but not manually forced.

Overflow:
- Positive `gate`: `exp(-gate)` underflows toward `0`, output tends to `gate * up`; safe.
- Negative `gate`: `exp(-gate)` can overflow if evaluated in fp16 for large negative gates, output tends to `0 * up`; mathematically silu is already tiny negative there.
- If TileLang lowers `T.exp` in fp32, overflow threshold is much farther out and correctness risk is low.
- OJ correctness is the hard gate because downstream GEMM may amplify many tiny activation differences.

**SHAPE_ISOLATION**

This is isolated to E64 because v160’s shape key includes `num_experts`, and separate generated kernels are cached per shape. E16 and E32 should compile and execute the same native-sigmoid path as v160.

Expected unchanged control shapes:
- E16: native `T.sigmoid`
- E32: native `T.sigmoid`

Only E64:
- explicit `T.exp`

**EXPECTED_SIGNAL**

Positive signal:
- E64 accepted and improves versus v160’s `6.557 ms`, or at least is neutral enough to justify further measurement.
- E16/E32 match v160 timing envelope because they are unchanged.

Negative signal:
- E64 correctness fail from overflow/rounding.
- E64 slower due to `T.exp` plus division lowering worse than native `T.sigmoid`.
- E16/E32 timing moves, indicating isolation failed.

**RISKS**

Main risks:
- `T.exp` may lower to a slower SFU sequence than native sigmoid on MACA.
- Division may be costlier than multiply with native sigmoid result.
- fp16 exp overflow for negative gates could exceed tolerance.
- Implicit constant promotion could introduce hidden fp32 work.
- Historical v041/v043 evidence is not performance-valid for current v160.

No approximation variants should follow unless this exact natural-exp primitive is positive.

**IMPLEMENTATION**

Implement as a one-file v168 copy from v160. Minimal code shape:

```python
def _swiglu_exp_kernel(...):
    ...
    activation[...] = (
        (gate / (1.0 + T.exp(-gate)))
        * up_output[expert_id, i, bn * block_n + j]
    )
```

Then in `run_kernel`:

```python
if num_experts == 64:
    swiglu_exp(gate_output, up_output, group_sizes, gate_output)
else:
    swiglu(gate_output, up_output, group_sizes, ...)
```

Current sandbox is read-only, so I did not create the v168 file here.

**FINAL_GATE**

PASS_TO_IMPLEMENT
